# Source link health, 2026-11-04

Generated by `node scripts/link-health.mjs` from `data/sources.json` and `data/evidence.json`; the machine-readable results are in [`link-health-2026-11-04.json`](link-health-2026-11-04.json). Nothing in `data/` was changed. Bodies are cached in the ignored `.local/link-health/` directory.

Coverage: 2033 of 2033 sources complete, 2201 distinct addresses requested.

## Method

Each source's `evidenceUrl` and `canonicalUrl` were requested once (once in all when they are the same), by GET with curl, one request at a time, at least 3 s apart per host, 30 s timeout per request, user agent `selekoa-link-health (source link and quotation check)`. Redirects were followed by hand, so the chain and final address are recorded. For each answer the status, content type, byte length and SHA-256 of the (decompressed) body are recorded. The requests went out from this Windows machine on its normal network, outside the sandbox; "blocked" and "unreachable" describe retrieval limits from this network, not proof that a document is broken or gone.

Every quotation (evidence with `passageType: quote`) was matched against the text extracted from the body: visible text first, then page source for HTML (some pages carry their article in a script blob), `pdftotext` for PDF (content-stream order, then reading order). Invisible format characters (bidi overrides that some PDF exporters wrap around each line, zero-width spaces, soft hyphens) are removed from the extracted text, and inline HTML tags are dropped without adding a space. **Verbatim** is an exact match; **normalised** folds whitespace, quotation marks and dashes only; anything else is **not found**. A not-found passage was then tried loosely (Unicode compatibility forms, CJK radical forms, case, soft and PDF line-end hyphens, control characters, marked elisions in order, and all whitespace and hyphens ignored); a loose hit is listed as a likely extraction artefact but not counted as a match.

| Class       | Meaning                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ----------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| ok          | Answered; every quotation found.                                                                                                                                            |
| changed     | Answered with readable text, but some quotations not found.                                                                                                                 |
| moved       | Redirected to a different document (not http→https, `www.` or a trailing slash).                                                                                            |
| gone        | HTTP 4xx (other than 401/403/429) or 5xx, or no DNS record.                                                                                                                 |
| blocked     | HTTP 401/403/429, a challenge page, or a refusal by this environment's proxy. Not circumvented.                                                                             |
| unreachable | Timeout, connection, DNS or certificate-validation failure; the curl detail is recorded.                                                                                    |
| unreadable  | Answered, but no text to check the quotations against: script-rendered page, unsupported type, or an HTML landing or viewer page in which no quotation occurs even loosely. |

A source takes the most severe class of its two addresses, in the order of the table (changed outranks blocked when one address was readable).

## Summary

### By class

| Class                                     | Sources |
| ----------------------------------------- | ------: |
| gone                                      |      23 |
| moved                                     |      81 |
| changed                                   |     251 |
| — of which a quotation not found at all   |      18 |
| — of which found only after loose folding |     233 |
| blocked                                   |      71 |
| unreachable                               |      19 |
| unreadable                                |      48 |
| ok                                        |    1540 |
| **Total**                                 |    2033 |

Quotations checked: 5901. Verbatim 4326, normalised 658, not found 917 (432 of them with a loose hit). Quotations of blocked, unreachable and unreadable sources are counted as not found here; the per-class lists below separate them.

## Reading and recommended next steps

<!-- notes:start -->

This is a fresh run from the maintainer's Windows machine on its normal network, outside the sandbox, using `node scripts/link-health.mjs --date 2026-11-04`. The interrupted sandbox attempt was discarded before this run. No source body or quotation was manually adjudicated, and no dataset record was changed.

The script's seven classes are retained. For the requested link-health totals, **failing means gone + unreachable (42)**; moved means redirected (81). The 251 changed and 48 unreadable sources remain separate quotation/extraction results, not confirmed broken links. One PDF (`c3-src-iflytek-sustainability-2025`) answered HTTP 200 but `pdftotext` failed; its unreadable result is retained.

Temporary redirect credentials were removed from both outputs after generation. The script's S3 filter missed Gitee signature queries, identity-provider codes, a WeChat challenge token, a Dropbox access-key query and a temporary Dropbox download path. The script itself is unchanged. Transient query fields were also removed where already present in an input citation address. These redactions preserve the hosts, stable paths where possible, classifications, hashes and request timestamps.

### Comparison with 2026-10-29

All 905 prior source IDs remain; 1128 sources are new. This is a comparison of recorded results, not an identical-input experiment: the dataset, citation routes and network differ. 532 shared sources gained an archivedUrl since October. Three recovered sources have changed citation routes: `source-faif-2026`, `source-hanson-1998-some-skepticism`, `source-hanson-2000-long-term-growth-exponential-modes`. Recovery does not establish that an old live URL now works or that a changed quotation has been editorially accepted.

- **Newly failing retrievals among shared sources (2):** `source-mi-hb4668-history` (ok → unreachable); `source-mi-hb4668-introduced` (ok → unreachable). Both Michigan legislative addresses failed to connect.
- **Newly recovered to ok:** 74, listed below with the previous class.
- **Still without archivedUrl:** 1245 current sources: 180 shared sources still lack one, plus 1065 newly added sources. These are missing dataset fields, not proof that no archive exists. This script does not independently request archivedUrl values unless they are also citation addresses.
- **Failing sources absent from the prior run:** 19, listed separately below; they cannot be called newly broken since October.

## Comparison with recorded dates and hashes

**Hashes.** 1437 requested addresses have a SHA-256 recorded for the same address in a pack or review file under `research/` or `review/`. 806 of them returned identical bytes today; 631 differ. A difference is expected for dynamic pages (scripts, timestamps, tracking) and for hashes taken of a compressed or differently negotiated body, so it is a prompt to look, not evidence of a changed text; the quotation check above is the substantive test.
